Indeed Help Compendium

Official Indeed help for job seekers and employers

2026-07-28

Indeed Help Compendium

This compendium contains the main content of 145 articles from Indeed’s official help: the Job Seeker Help Center at support.indeed.com (which currently publishes its articles across a small set of topics; Indeed has been moving much job seeker guidance into in-product pages) and the English edition of the Employer Help Center. Job seeker articles are grouped under the Help Center’s own topics; employer articles appear under a single Employers section. Titles are alphabetized within each section (ignoring leading words such as About and How to). Interactive contact forms are not articles and are not included. Site chrome and feedback prompts have been removed.

Employers

About Apply Start

Apply start shows how many people take the first step to apply for your jobs. Tracking it helps you gauge interest and find drop‑offs so you can simplify the process.

  • Jan 27, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

Apply-start-overview

Title

About Apply Start

Details

Overview

Apply start is the first step a job seeker takes to apply for a job on Indeed. It happens when they click the Apply now button on a job post. This shows a strong interest in the job, even if the person does not finish the full application.

You can find apply start metrics in Indeed’s analytics.

Benefits

Measure performance
  • Accurate and reliable metrics
  • One step closer from click to hire
  • Captures high-intent job seekers
Track more accurately
  • Not affected by third-party cookie blocking
  • Works better than traditional conversion tracking

Finding your apply start metrics

Visit the Jobs and campaigns tab of your Analytics page for:

  • Apply start percentage
  • The total number of apply starts
  • Cost per apply start
  • Apply start engagement summary
  • Apply start rate for your highest and lowest-ranked jobs

Apply starts vs applications

You may find that the number of applications for a job exceeds the number of apply starts. There might be two reasons for this discrepancy:

  • The job seeker applied without clicking Apply now, for example, by using a direct link to the application.
  • The job seeker began the application process a day or more before completing it. To prevent this discrepancy, look at the data for a longer period.

Troubleshooting

Low click-to-apply start ratio
Issue: Jobseekers click but don’t start applying.

Solution: Improve job descriptions to drive interest.

  • The job description may lack key details.
  • The job title doesn’t match the job description.
Low apply start-to-apply ratio
Issue: Job seekers start but don’t complete applications.

Solution: Shorten and simplify the application process.

  • The application process may be too long or complex.

About Billback and Billing Identifiers

Learn how to use billing identifiers on Indeed to track job posting costs by your internal categories like department, cost center, or location. This guide covers creating and managing billing identifiers, assigning them to jobs, and downloading detailed billback reports to analyze spending.

  • Apr 21, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

About-Billback-and-Billing-Identifiers

Title

About Billback and Billing Identifiers

Details

Overview

Billing identifiers are client-defined codes (department, cost center, location, region, etc.) that can be attached to jobs on Indeed. Using billing identifiers helps you to track spending by your own internal categories.

Who can use billing identifiers?

Important: This feature may not be available for you. For more information, reach out to your Indeed rep.

This feature may be available to you if you:

  • Post jobs directly on Indeed

Contact your Client Success Rep if you:

  • Send jobs to Indeed through an ATS, API, XML feed, or similar method, and wish to automate the billing identifier population
  • Tip: You can manually add identifiers, but we recommend reaching out to your CS representative to help automatically populate them

Creating billing identifiers

  1. Sign in to your Indeed account.
  2. Navigate to the Jobs page.
  3. Click or tap the Managed on Indeed tab at the top.
  4. Select the jobs you want to group under the same billing identifier.
    1. You can only add one billing identifier per job.
  5. At the top right, click or tap the three dots and choose the Billing identifier dropdown.
  6. Click or tap Create billing identifier.
  7. Enter a billing identifier name that works for you (for example, a cost center, department, or location name).
  8. Click or tap Create billing identifier.
  9. Check that the right jobs are selected under this new billing identifier, then click or tap Add.
    1. You can add more jobs to this billing identifier at any time later.

The billing identifier will now show under each job on your Jobs page.

Adding additional jobs to existing identifiers
  1. On your Jobs page, select the jobs you want to add.
  2. At the top right, click or tap the three dots and choose the Billing identifier dropdown.
  3. Click or tap Add billing identifier.
  4. Choose the correct billing identifier from the dropdown.

The selected jobs will now be linked to that billing identifier.

Managing billing identifiers

Editing billing identifiers for one or multiple jobs
  1. Sign in to your Indeed account.
  2. Navigate to the Jobs page.
  3. Click or tap the Managed on Indeed tab at the top.
  4. Select the jobs you want to group under the same billing identifier.
    1. You can only add one billing identifier per job.
  5. At the top right, click or tap the three dots and choose the Billing identifier dropdown.
    1. For multiple jobs:Select all the jobs you want to update, then click Add Billing Identifier. This allows you to edit the billing identifier for all selected jobs at once.
    2. For a single job:Click or tap the three dots next to the job, then select Edit Billing Identifier to update it for that job only.
Removing a billing identifier for a job
  1. Navigate to the Jobs page.
  2. Click or tap the Managed on Indeed tab at the top.
  3. Find the job with the billing identifier you want to remove.
  4. Click the existing billing identifier and select Remove billing identifier.
  5. Save your changes. The job will no longer show that billing identifier.

Once removed, you can add a new billing identifier for the job.

Downloading a billback report

Once billing identifiers are set, view cost breakdowns in Billing Reports.

  1. Visit the Billing and invoices page.
  2. Select the Reports tab.
  3. The report shows a cost breakdown by billing identifier and detailed job-level spends.
    1. The default setting shows the last 3 months.
    2. Change the date range to filter for a specific timeframe.

Important: If the report shows the billing identifier “NA”, it means that the billing identifier did not exist for that job.

About Employer Branding Ads

Learn how Employer Branding Ads (EBA) help you reach and attract high-quality job seekers across Indeed, Glassdoor, and off-site platforms through targeted display advertising, including four ad format options (desktop and mobile-optimized with varying image specifications), optional video ads (15-60 seconds), multiple landing page choices (Glassdoor Enhanced Profile, Indeed Company Page, or external careers page), targeting by location/search behavior/resume, and performance tracking through Indeed Analytics.

  • Nov 18, 2024
  • Knowledge

URL Name

What-are-Employer-Branding-Ads

Title

About Employer Branding Ads

Details

Overview

Employer Branding Ads (EBA) let you reach high-quality job seekers through your brand story. These ads run across Indeed, Glassdoor, and off-site to let candidates learn more about your company and consider applying.

Purpose

In one simple process, you can target job seekers by location, search behavior, and resume to:

  • Boost awareness and consideration
  • Promote your company in new locations
  • Attract candidates for functions your company isn’t known for

How do Employer Branding Ads work?

Employer Branding Ads work by placing desktop- and mobile-optimized display ads on Indeed, Glassdoor, and off-site to re-target recent job seekers.

Off-site ads are shown to relevant users browsing sites outside of the Indeed platform. This helps you extend your reach beyond Indeed.

When a job seeker clicks on an Employer Branding Ad, they will be driven to your Branded Company Page (Glassdoor), Company Page (Indeed), or your company’s own careers page to help you tell your brand story.

Note: If you want to set up an Employer Branding Ad, please connect with your Glassdoor Sales Representative.

What do Employer Branding Ads look like?

Employer Branding Ads will appear in four formats. Based on your target audience, we will match ads with job seekers in a blend of these four formats for optimal reach across mobile and desktop.

  • Ad Type: Format One; Ad Location: - Indeed - Off-site - Facebook and Instagram - Glassdoor; Content Needed: - Header image (1400 x 1400px) - Brand text (95 characters maximum) - Call-to-action text (30 characters maximum) - Your click-through URL (Indeed, Glassdoor, or external website)
  • Ad Type: Format Two; Ad Location: - Glassdoor - Off-site; Content Needed: - 300 x 250px image in jpg, jpeg, or png file formats - Your click-through URL (Indeed, Glassdoor, or external website)
  • Ad Type: Format Three; Ad Location: - Glassdoor; Content Needed: - Cover photo image (1440 x 275px in jpg, jpeg, or png file formats)
  • Ad Type: Format Four; Ad Location: - Glassdoor; Content Needed: - 1000 x 750px image or 500 x 375px image in jpg, jpeg, or png file formats (100kb maximum) - ID to a Featured Review (if not provided, a five star review will be sourced from your profile)

Note: Third-party impression tracking is supported for each of the four formats above when you provide an optional impression pixel.

Adding video to Employer Branding Ads

You can separately purchase EBA with video to add video to your Employer Branding Ads.

While Indeed does not offer a self-serve video ad option, you can create your video content and then contact your Indeed or Glassdoor representative to set up your campaign.

Videos cannot be longer than one minute. Additional specifications are listed below for your video ads:

  • File size: Video aspect ratio; 25MB maximum: Aspect Ratio: 16:9, 4:3 (3:2 is not accepted)
  • File size: Video size; 25MB maximum: 1280x720, 1920x1080, or 1440x1080
  • File size: Video bitrate; 25MB maximum: 15-30 Mbps (CBR)
  • File size: Video frame rate; 25MB maximum: 23.98, 25, or 29.97
  • File size: Video length; 25MB maximum: 15, 30, or 60 seconds max animation times - Recommend 15 or 30
  • File size: File type; 25MB maximum: Quicktime (.mov), Mpeg-4 (.mp4) or flv
  • File size: Video playback rate; 25MB maximum: The platform we use transcodes raw video files for best performance on publisher sites.
  • File size: Audio bitrate; 25MB maximum: 192 kbps minimum
  • File size: Audio sample rate; 25MB maximum: 48 kHz
  • File size: Audio code; 25MB maximum: H.264
  • File size: Click-through URL; 25MB maximum: Videos will click through on supported inventory. Some publishers do not support clicks on ads. Options for landing pages are the same as static Employer Branding Ads.

Tracking performance of Employer Branding Ads

  1. Sign in to your Indeed account.
  2. Go to Analyticsand select Employer Branding Ads at the top of the screen.

Employer Branding Ads are also available through a subscription to Employer Branding Suite.

About Indeed and Glassdoor

This article explains how Indeed and Glassdoor work together to help employers reach more job seekers, improve application completion, and build an employer brand through shared job ad visibility and a single job seeker login. Covers multiple ways this works, including Indeed job ads appearing on Glassdoor, candidates applying via Indeed Apply on Glassdoor, optional profile connection for sourcing, and the fact that employer accounts, billing, and permissions remain separate.

  • Jun 26, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

Indeed-and-Glassdoor-Partnership

Title

About Indeed and Glassdoor

Details

Overview

Indeed and Glassdoor work together to help you reach more job seekers and build your employer brand. Job seekers can use one login to access both sites. This can make applying faster and help you connect with more relevant candidates. Employer accounts, billing, and permissions remain separate.

Benefits

  • Reach more people on both sites with your Indeed job ads.
  • Find better matches as more job seekers connect their profiles.
  • Improve completion and response rates with a smoother application process and candidate follow-up.

Features

Job ads on both sites
  • Indeed job ads can appear on Indeed and Glassdoor when eligible.
  • You still set up campaigns and pay through Indeed.
  • Reports stay in your Indeed tools.
Better connections with candidates
  • Many job seekers now use one login for both sites.
  • They can apply using Indeed Apply on Glassdoor jobs.
  • If they choose to opt in, you can find them using sourcing tools.

Important: It’s the job seeker’s choice to connect profiles or be discoverable. Employers do not control this setting.

FAQs

Question: Are employer accounts combined for Indeed and Glassdoor?

Answer: No. Accounts and permissions stay separate. You should keep using each site’s employer login.

Question: Will this help my job ad performance?

Answer: Maybe. Showing jobs on both sites and making it easier to apply can lead to more completed and relevant applications over time.

About Indeed Company Pages

This article explains how Indeed Company Pages help employers showcase their brand, culture, and jobs to attract talent, and details how to create or claim a page, manage reviews, add salaries, and engage with candidates through Q&A and interviews. It compares Free vs. Premium Company Page features, covers Glassdoor integration, Work Wellbeing Scores, and eligibility for standalone pages, while also answering common FAQs on merging pages, reviews, and salary data.

  • Sep 12, 2025
  • Knowledge

URL Name

204747960

Title

About Indeed Company Pages

Details

Overview

An Indeed Company Page helps you show your brand, culture, and jobs to millions of job seekers. When people research your company, your page gives them a clear picture of what it’s like to work with you.

You can use your Company Page to:

  • Share your story and workplace culture
  • Build trust with reviews, salaries, and job postings
  • Attract qualified candidates

Creating and claiming your Company Page

  1. Post a job on Indeed.
  2. Once your job is live, a Company Page is created automatically.
  3. Claim your page to edit content, reply to reviews, and share branding. Learn more about editing your Company Page.

Important: Contact us if your page isn’t created automatically.

What job seekers see on your Company Page

  • Snapshot: Key details about your company

  • Why Join Us: Your story, culture, and values

  • Reviews: Ratings and comments from employees

  • Salaries: Pay information shared by employees

  • Jobs: Linked to your page when your company name matches your Company Page branding. This may include jobs:

    • From your Applicant Tracking System (ATS)
    • You post on Indeed
    • Found online

  • Q&A: Job seekers can ask questions. Your replies show as coming from the company.

  • Interviews: Insights into your hiring process.

Tip: Replying to reviews and Q&A shows candidates you value feedback and transparency.

Your Company Page features

The features available depend on the type of page you have:

Free Company Page
  • Engage with potential job seekers.
  • Promote open jobs.
  • Share company updates.
Premium Company Page

Premium pages include additional tools to strengthen your employer brand:

  • Branded About page with custom design options

  • Company tab on mobile and desktop

  • Featured reviews

  • Advanced analytics, including:

    • Detailed branding and engagement metrics
    • Review and rating filters
    • Competitor comparisons

  • Integrate social media feeds and links

  • A custom header for your Company Page

Glassdoor integration

Employer Branding Hub clients can link their Indeed and Glassdoor accounts. With this integration, you can:

  • Manage Indeed and Glassdoor branding in one place
  • Respond to Indeed reviews
  • View ratings by title and location
  • Track impressions and page views across both platforms
Work Wellbeing Score

If your company collects enough survey responses, a Work Wellbeing Score appears on your page. It shows job seekers employee happiness, purpose, satisfaction, and stress.

Learn more about the Work Wellbeing Score.

Standalone Company Pages

Some companies may qualify for their own standalone Company Page. Indeed decides if you qualify, and you may need Indeed premium service.

You may be eligible if your company is:

  • Part of a franchise group with its own brand or name
  • A cooperative location within a larger group
  • A product distributor for another company
  • Part of an automotive dealership
  • An authorized mobile retailer
  • A professional sports team or religious group
  • A media outlet within a larger group
  • A government body or political office
  • A representative of a contractor or 1099 organization
  • A hospital within a larger healthcare organization

Learn more about Indeed’s Company Page Policy.

FAQs

Question: My business has more than one Company Page. Can I merge them?

Answer: Maybe. Contact us if you notice several Company Pages for your business.

Question: Can I remove reviews from my page?

Answer: No. Reviews stay up unless they violate our content guidelines. You can respond professionally or report the review if it doesn’t follow our guidelines.

Question: Can I hide my Work Wellbeing Score?

Answer: No. This score shows once your company receives enough survey responses.

Question: The salary data on my page looks wrong. Can I edit it?

Answer: No. Salary information is based on averages from employee reviews and job postings. You can’t remove it, but you can influence it by adding accurate salaries to your job posts and encouraging employees to share their pay in reviews.

Important: Contact us if salary information on your page is clearly inaccurate or unrelated to your company. We’ll review the data and remove it if necessary.

About Indeed Employer Single Sign-On (SSO)

Learn how Indeed Employer Single Sign-On (SSO) works, including who is eligible, the security and access benefits, and how to get started by contacting Indeed support for guided setup or using a self-paced implementation option when available.

  • Jan 29, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

Indeed-Employer-Single-Sign-On-SSO-Overview

Title

About Indeed Employer Single Sign-On (SSO)

Details

Overview

Single Sign-On (SSO) lets your employees sign in to Indeed using their work login. This means they don’t need a separate username, password, or extra security steps. SSO helps protect your account and makes it easier for employees to access Indeed.

Purpose

SSO helps you manage account access in a simple and secure way. Employees use their company login to sign in to Indeed. This can bypass two-factor authentication (2FA) and government ID checks. SSO helps reduce login problems and improve security. It can also remove access for former employees automatically.

Benefits

  • Better security: Employees use trusted work credentials.
  • Easy access: No extra usernames or passwords to remember.
  • Less work for admins: It’s simple to add and manage users, especially when many employees join or leave.
  • Simplify access control: Get employees the right permissions in a few steps.

Important: You may still need to use direct login for certain features, like accessing a Company Page, even after SSO is set up.

Getting started

Step 1: Contact Indeed support

You can contact us by chat, phone, or message.

Step 2: Eligibility check

We’ll confirm if SSO is available and a good fit for your company.

Step 3: Pre-implementation meeting (30 minutes)

We review technical details, plan next steps, and schedule the implementation.

Step 4: Implementation meeting (30-60 minutes)

We complete the setup and prepare for launch. Some employers may use a self-paced setup instead.

Important: Your HR or Talent Acquisition (TA) admin and your IT admin must join the setup process.

More information for IT teams

How SSO works

We support SP-initiated SSO by default. IdP-initiated SSO is also available if needed.

Supported protocols

SAML and OIDC (SCIM is not supported.)

Testing before launch

We can test SSO with a small group of users before going live.

Domain Enforcement for SSO Users

Domain Enforcement adds security by limiting access to approved company email addresses. Users must have an approved email domain (like @yourcompany.com) and sign in through your company’s SSO system.

How to turn on and edit Domain Enforcement

Follow these steps to enable Domain Enforcement:

  1. Go to your Employer Settings
  2. Select Edit domains to add or remove domains
  3. Select Edit policies and toggle on domain enforcement for the domains listed.

About Indeed Hiring Events

This article explains how Indeed Hiring Events help employers interview many candidates at once—virtually or in person—by using built-in tools to screen applicants, manage RSVPs, schedule interviews, and track hiring outcomes in one dashboard. It guides users through multiple ways to run a successful event, including preparing and promoting events, using basic or advanced screening, integrating an ATS, managing interviews and candidate communications, and reviewing performance, feedback, and hires after the event.

  • Jun 24, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

Best-practices-for-Indeed-Hiring-Events

Title

About Indeed Hiring Events

Details

Overview

Indeed Hiring Events help you interview many people in one place. You can host interviews online or in person. The platform helps you screen candidates, schedule interviews, and track results. Below, we cover what Hiring Events are, why they help, and how to run one.

Purpose

Indeed Hiring Events make hiring faster and easier. They bring candidates and interviewers together at one time. Built-in tools help you screen candidates and schedule interviews.

How it works

  • Create a hiring event for one or more jobs.
  • Candidates RSVP and answer screening questions.
  • During the event, you interview candidates online or in person.
  • After the event, you review feedback and track hires in one dashboard.

Getting started

Prepare your event.
  • Decide how many people you need to hire and by when.
  • Choose virtual or in-person interviews.
  • Post your job on Indeed to simplify editing and updating roles
  • Set RSVP limits based on how many interviews you can handle.
  • Create an automated message for candidates who RSVP.

Tip: Connect your applicant tracking system (ATS) to automatically collect RSVPs and applications.

Set screening preferences.
  • Use screener questions to remove candidates who don’t meet your needs.
Promote your event.
  • Share your event on social media like Facebook or LinkedIn.
  • Check results to confirm if you’re getting the right number of candidates.

Running the event

Prepare for the interviews.
For in-person events
  • Use the check-in tool to collect resumes and candidate details.
For virtual events
  • Use the All Hiring Events tab to review and join interviews.
  • Check your account settings and lobby access at least one day before the event.
  • Test your camera and microphone.

Learn more about:

  • Running an interview event on Indeed
  • Virtual interviews
Manage your event and candidates.
  • Edit the event or pause spending from your dashboard.
  • Send bulk emails to candidates who signed up.

After the interviews

In the Hiring Events dashboard, you can:

  • Rate the experience
  • Review contact details
  • Track offers and hire
  • Review event performance

Resources

About Indeed’s Audience Network

This article explains how Indeed’s Audience Network expands the reach of Sponsored Jobs by automatically showing eligible ads on select partner sites—such as DSP and social placements—in addition to Indeed, Glassdoor, and the Trusted Media Network. It helps employers understand how off-site eligibility works, how targeting and pricing function (PPSA or PPC), and how performance is reported within standard Sponsored Jobs metrics.

  • Dec 4, 2025
  • Knowledge

URL Name

Amplify-reach-with-Indeed-s-Audience-Network

Title

About Indeed’s Audience Network

Details

Overview

Indeed’s Audience Network helps more people see your Sponsored Jobs. It shows your job ads on select partner sites outside of Indeed and Glassdoor. This helps you reach job seekers who may not be visiting our sites at that moment. Only some Sponsored Jobs are eligible to appear off-site. Your Sponsored Jobs metrics include all performance from these ads.

Purpose

The Audience Network helps you reach more of the right job seekers. It uses the same signals we use on Indeed and Glassdoor, such as a job seeker’s location, activity, and resume data. This helps maintain quality and ensures consistent targeting. You don’t need to do anything; Indeed decides when off-site ads can help your campaign.

Benefits

  • Reach active and passive job seekers across many partner sites and channels.
  • Use the same high-quality audience signals used for onsite ads.
  • Review your Sponsored Jobs metrics for performance.
  • No setup needed; Indeed handles eligibility.

How it works

Post and sponsor a job
  1. Create a job post as usual.
  2. Choose your budget and goal.
  3. Your sponsored job becomes eligible for off-site placement if it meets our criteria.
Let Indeed handle targeting
  • Indeed reviews your job, your campaign goal, and job seeker signals.
  • We show your ad to people who are likely to be a good match.
  • We use the same first-party signals used on Indeed and Glassdoor. These include location, behavior, and experience.
Reach job seekers across partner sites
  • Some ads may appear on third-party placements. These can be:
    • Sites reached through a large demand-side platform like Beeswax
    • Social channels like Meta
    • These are examples only; partners can change over time.
  • Your sponsored job may appear on these sites if it:
    • Meets the criteria for eligibility
    • Helps your campaign perform well
Understand pricing
Pay per started application (PPSA)
  • You pay when a job seeker starts the application.
  • Learn more about PPSA.
Pay per click (PPC)
  • You pay when a job seeker clicks the job title to view the job.
  • Learn more about PPC advertising.

Important: Offsite clicks or started applications may cost more than onsite ones. When onsite delivery is more efficient, Indeed will focus on onsite traffic.

FAQs

Question: Will all my Sponsored Jobs appear in the Audience Network?

Answer: No. Only a small number of Sponsored Jobs are eligible for off-site placements.

Question: Do I need to turn on the Audience Network?

Answer: No. Indeed handles eligibility and off-site distribution for you.

Question: Can I see Audience Network results in a separate report?

Answer: No. Your Sponsored Jobs metrics include off-site performance.

Question: Will my ads still appear on Indeed and Glassdoor?

Answer: Yes. Your Sponsored Jobs can appear in several places at once. These include Indeed, Glassdoor, the Trusted Media Network, and the Audience Network.

About Itemized Reports

This article explains what an itemized report is, who can access it, and how to download it from your Indeed Employer account. Learn how to view, understand, and export detailed spending data for Sponsored Jobs or Employer Branding Ads (EBA) as a CSV or PDF file.

  • Nov 4, 2025
  • Knowledge

URL Name

How-to-download-an-itemized-report-for-sponsored-jobs

Title

About Itemized Reports

Details

Overview

An itemized report shows how your job ad or campaign budget was spent. If you use Sponsored Jobs or are an Employer Branding Ads (EBA) client, you may be able to download one along with your invoice.

Each report lists your spending for every job or campaign, along with key details like costs, impressions, and purchase order numbers.

Purpose

Itemized reports help you check exactly where your money goes. They make it easier to review charges, track job ad performance, and keep your billing records organized. You can also use the report to share cost data with your finance or reporting teams.

Benefits

Use the itemized report to:

  • Understand how costs were split across jobs or campaigns
  • Track key actions, such as impressions or applications
  • Match charges to purchase orders or insertion orders (POs or IOs)
  • Review and confirm total spend for your records
  • Export data for deeper analysis in spreadsheets or reports

Features

What’s included in an itemized report

Each report includes:

  • Company name
  • Campaign name and ID
  • Impressions: How many times your job ad was seen (if applicable)
  • IO: The insertion order linked to the campaign
  • PO: The purchase order number, if added when the IO was signed
  • Unit: The billing type (for example, Impressions or Started Application)
  • CPM: Cost per thousand impressions (for impression-based campaigns)
  • Total spend: How much was spent per job or campaign
  • Currency: The currency used on the invoice

At the bottom of the report, you’ll also find:

  • Total cost
  • Tax
  • Total amount charged
Who can access itemized reports

You can download an itemized report if:

  • You’re using Sponsored Jobs or are an EBA client
  • You’re not on a Prepay IO model
  • Your invoice was created after May 2023

Downloading your itemized report

  1. Sign in to your employer account on Indeed.
  2. Click or tap your email address in the top-right corner.
  3. Select Billing and invoices from the menu.
  4. Scroll to the Transaction history section.
  5. If available, click Download under the Itemized report column.

You can download the report as a CSV (spreadsheet) or a PDF file.

About Pay Per Started Application (PPSA)

This article explains how Indeed’s Pay Per Started Application (PPSA) pricing model for Sponsored Jobs works, helping employers pay only when a job seeker begins an application and optimize hiring through dynamic bidding, higher-intent candidate reach, and multi-platform promotion. It also outlines how PPSA is automatically enabled, how budgets function, and clarifies options such as direct job posting, ATS integration, and Smart Sourcing (which does not incur PPSA charges).

  • May 4, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

Pay-Per-Started-Application-PPSA-Overview

Title

About Pay Per Started Application (PPSA)

Details

Overview

Pay Per Started Application (PPSA) is Indeed’s results-based pricing model for Sponsored Jobs. With PPSA, you pay only when a job seeker starts an application for your job, not when they click your ad.

Purpose

PPSA helps you focus your budget on real results by paying only for started applications from interested job seekers. Use PPSA to fill roles faster, reach higher-intent candidates, and optimize performance.

What is a started application?

A started application is when a job seeker takes an action to begin the application process. This may include:

  • Clicking an apply button (for example, Apply now or Apply on company site)
  • Opening a link to start or continue an application
  • Using a chatbot or QR code to begin applying
  • Scheduling an interview when that step replaces the application form

Check Indeed’s Terms of Service for full details.

Benefits

  • Pay only for started applications, not clicks.
  • Reach higher-intent candidates.
  • Optimise performance with dynamic bidding.
  • Fill roles faster and more efficiently.
  • Promote jobs across multiple platforms.
  • Manage campaigns easily through one dashboard.

Features

Pay for performance.
  • Pay for started applications, not just clicks.
  • Adjusts bids in real time to help deliver more started applications.
Fill your roles efficiently.
  • Focus on candidates who show a stronger interest.
  • Manage campaigns through one dashboard.
Meet your hiring objectives.
  • Promote your roles across Indeed, Glassdoor, and the Trusted Media Network.
  • Tailor objective-based campaigns to meet your hiring goals.

Using PPSA

Who can use PPSA?
  • PPSA is available for most Sponsored Jobs.
  • You can use PPSA if you:
    • Post jobs directly on Indeed or through your career site or Applicant Tracking System (ATS).
    • Sponsor your jobs as a paid campaign.
How do you turn on PPSA?
  • Your Sponsored Jobs campaign will automatically use PPSA.
  • You’ll only pay when a job seeker starts an application, not when they click your job.
Understanding PPSA campaign budgets
  • PPSA uses a live auction model, so costs depend on candidate demand.
  • Your budget may be spent faster or slower as Indeed drives more started applications.
  • We recommend working with your Indeed rep to set a budget that supports strong results.
  • You may see a minimum budget per job for Objective-based Campaigns.
  • These minimums help ensure budgets are high enough to deliver more started applications.
  • Budget minimums vary by market, so speak with your Indeed rep if you have questions.

FAQs

Question: Can I opt out of PPSA?

Answer: No. Qualifying jobs are automatically enrolled in PPSA pricing. You cannot switch back to Pay Per Click (PPC).

Question: Will I get charged for applications started through Smart Sourcing?

Answer: No. Applications from Smart Sourcing outreach are not charged to your PPSA sponsorship budget.

Recruiting Assistant uses AI tools to help you find, review, and focus on candidates. In this article, we’ll help you get started with Premium Plus and set up your Assistant.

Important: Premium Plus is only available for U.S. jobs posted on Indeed.

Purpose

Premium Plus is for employers who want more help finding and reviewing candidates. It includes Recruiting Assistant, which uses AI to help with sourcing and screening. This can save time on recruiting tasks. It can also help you find strong matches faster.

Benefits

  • Get all Premium features, plus Recruiting Assistant.
  • Save time on finding, screening, and sorting candidates.
  • Reach and connect with potential candidates more easily.
  • Review applicants using screening scores and summaries.

Features

All Premium features

Learn more about Premium Sponsored Jobs.

Recruiting Assistant features
Auto-invite matched candidates
  • Use hiring criteria to auto-invite up to 30 candidates per day.

Automated candidate outreach
  • Send messages based on the candidate’s screening score.

  5. Review your screening settings.
  6. Select Confirm to complete setup.
Managing Recruiting Assistant
  1. Open your Jobs dashboard.
  2. Select Recruiting assistant for the job you want to edit.
  3. Update your Assistant instructions.
  4. Give feedback on match quality. This helps improve future results.

FAQs

Answer: No. You’ll have to remove the job from the campaign and sponsor it on its own. Find out how to edit your campaign to remove a job.

About the Indeed Hiring Events Dashboard

Overview

The Hiring Events dashboard is where you manage all your Hiring Events. It lists each event and its current status. You can review details such as dates, locations, and job titles. You can also take action to edit, join, or manage events at different stages. This article explains the different functions available in your dashboard.

Accessing your dashboard

Once your Hiring Event is set up, you can access your dashboard in three different ways.

From your employer account
  1. Sign in to your Indeed account.
  2. Select Interviews in the left sidebar.

Columns and tabs
Status
  • Review current status and take action as needed

Title

Accessing Your Billing and Invoice Details

Details

Overview

This article is about the Billing and invoices page on your employer account. Learn what you can do in Billing and invoices, how invoices are created, and what you’ll find in the transaction history column. We’ll also explain how to get started, find and download invoices, and update your billing details. Check out the FAQs below if you have questions about the taxes applied to Indeed’s products, and what to do if you’re tax-exempt.

Important: Only users with billing permissions can access the Billing and invoices page. Learn more in Adding Users and Setting Access Levels.

About the Billing and invoices page

What you can do
  • Update billing contacts and payment methods
  • Access and download invoices
  • Track outstanding balances
    • Learn more about how to pay your Indeed invoices.
  • Get a summary of charges
  • Manage insertion orders, if applicable

Important: Contact Indeed support if you get an error while fetching data.

Invoice availability
  • Invoices for the previous billing period are available in the first few days of each month.
  • Each invoice is created once per billing cycle.
  • Invoices can’t be changed after they’re issued.

Learn more about when and why you get invoices from Indeed.

The transaction history columns
Date

The invoice date

Activity

The transaction type:

  • Invoice
  • Invoice adjustment
  • Payment
  • Refund
Product/Service

The product or service charged

Amount (currency)

The transaction amount:

  • Red: Invoice and Refund
  • Black: Payment and Invoice adjustment
Itemized report
  • Shows detailed charges for Sponsored Jobs or Employer Branding Ads.
  • Learn more in Downloading an Itemized Report.

Getting started

  1. Sign in to your Indeed account.
  2. Click or tap your email in the top right corner.
  3. Select Billing and invoices from the menu.

Finding and downloading invoices

Search for your invoices
  1. Go to the Billing and invoices page.
  2. Select the Summary tab
  3. Scroll to Transaction history.
  4. Under Invoices, you’ll find a list of invoices by month.
Filter transactions

Use the filters to sort by date or activity type:

  • Payments show payments and invoice adjustments
  • Invoices show invoices and refunds
Download your invoice
  1. Click or tap the invoice link in the Activity column.
  2. The PDF will include:
    1. Invoice number and date
    2. Due date
    3. Description or memo
    4. Amount
    5. Applicable products, taxes, promotions, and discounts

Editing your billing details

  1. Go to the Billing and invoices page.
  2. Select the Information tab.
  3. Click or tap Edit next to:
    1. Billing contact
    2. Billing information
    3. Payment method
  4. Make your changes.
  5. Click or tap Update.

FAQs

Question: Can I make changes to my last invoice?

Answer: No. Invoices can’t be updated once they’re issued. Edits to your billing details will apply to future invoices.

Question: The calculated taxes look wrong. Can I fix this?

Answer: Yes. Taxes are based on your billing details. Check that your billing address is correct. Updates will apply to future invoices.

Question: I have tax questions during an audit. Can you help?

Answer: Yes. Email salestax@indeed.com for help.

Question: I’m tax-exempt. Do I need to take action?

Answer: Yes. Send a copy of your exemption certificate to salestax@indeed.com.

Adding Users and Setting Access Levels

Learn how to add, edit, or remove users on your Indeed employer account by managing access levels from the Users page, including assigning permissions, updating roles, and securely collaborating without sharing login credentials.

  • May 12, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

206589143

Title

Adding Users and Setting Access Levels

Details

Overview

You must have administrator-level access in order to add and remove users on your account. If you are unsure if you have the right access, reach out to your account owner.

Add users to your Indeed employer account so team members can collaborate within the platform. Users will have their own login information, and you’ll control the access levels. For security, it’s best to add your team members as users on your employer account rather than sharing a single email and password.

Important: Contact us if you need to change or remove the account owner.

How to add users and set access levels

Adding a user
  1. Sign in to your employer account.
  2. Go to the Users page.
  3. Select Add users.
  4. Enter your team members’ email addresses. If you add someone who doesn’t have an Indeed account, they’ll be prompted to create one.
  5. Select All options.
  6. Select their access level.
  7. Save and notify new users.

Tip: Access levels are explained under All options if you need to review them.

Editing user access
  1. Go to the Users page.
  2. Find the user you want to edit.
  3. Select the three dots in the Actions column.
  4. Edit and select their access level.
  5. Save.
Removing users
  1. Go to the Users page.
  2. Find the user you want to edit.
  3. Select the three dots in the Actions column.
  4. Select Remove and Yes, remove.
Removing yourself as a user
  1. Go to the Users page.
  2. Find your user.
  3. Select the three dots in the Actions column.
  4. Select Remove yourself.

Note: Users can remove themselves from an advertiser if they are a secondary user. Users are not notified when their access level is changed or when they are removed.

Adding Users to an Indeed Candidate Conversation

Learn how to manage access to candidate conversations on Indeed by adding team members as full account users, granting job-level email access, or inviting participants to specific candidate message threads, and resolve common messaging errors or bouncebacks.

  • Mar 25, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

Unable-to-add-people-to-an-Indeed-conversation-with-a-candidate

Title

Adding Users to an Indeed Candidate Conversation

Details

Overview

Indeed limits who can join candidate conversations. This helps protect job seekers’ privacy and your company’s data. You can add team members to a conversation in two ways:

  • As full users of your company’s Indeed account
  • As participants in specific job or candidate conversations

If you can’t add someone or get an error message, it likely means they don’t have the right access.

This article shows how to give the correct access and fix common messaging issues.

Actions

Give full access to all candidate conversations

You can let users view and message candidates across all jobs in your Indeed account. They still need to join individual conversations to get updates and replies.

Who can do this

Admin-level users.

What to do
  • Add team members as Candidates or Admin users to the employer account
  • For steps, check our article How To Add Users and Set Access Levels
Give email access for a specific job

You can let someone get application updates and join conversations for one job. They must reply to a job application email to join the conversation.

Important:They don’t need an Indeed account to receive these updates.

Who can do this
  • Admin-level users
  • Users with job access permissions
What to do
  1. Sign in to the Indeed employer account used to contact candidates.
  2. Open the Jobs tab on the left side of the page.
  3. Select the job you want to update.
  4. Click or tap Edit job.
  5. Scroll down to Application updates.
  6. Click or tap + Add email to add the new conversation participant’s email address.

Anyone added here will receive application notifications for this job. If they reply to one of those emails, their reply goes to the conversation group email for that candidate and joins them to the conversation.

Adding someone to a specific candidate conversation group

You can add someone to a conversation thread with a specific candidate. They’ll get all future messages in that thread.

Important:They don’t need an Indeed account to be added as a participant.

Who can do this
  • Admin-level users
  • Linked users with Candidates access
What to do
  1. Sign in to the Indeed employer account used to contact candidates.
  2. Click or tap Messages at the top of the page.
  3. Select the conversation where you want to add a participant.
  4. Click or tap the participants icon (the people icon showing the number of participants).
  5. Add the new participant’s email address.

The new participant will receive future messages for that conversation. Removing a participant does not change their underlying user permissions on your account.

Exporting Email Addresses to Third-Party Systems

Indeed sends job application emails and messages from an address that won’t work with third-party software systems like your ATS or scheduling software.

To email candidates from one of those tools, use the candidate’s email alias instead.

You can find it:

  • On their resume
  • Or by using the bulk export feature

Important: Emails sent to a candidate’s email alias are email-only and don’t sync back to Indeed messaging.

Troubleshooting messaging errors and bouncebacks

Error: “Indeed couldn’t deliver this message”

Issue:You tried replying to a group conversation via email, but you don’t have Candidates access to the job.

Solution: Ask an Admin-level user to give you Candidates access for this job.

Error: “The following people were not added to your conversation with [candidate name]”

Issue: You CC’d someone who doesn’t have messaging access. They also don’t have an Indeed account linked to your company.

Solution: Ask an Admin-level user to give that person Candidates access for this job.

Adult Industry Jobs

This article explains Indeed’s adult industry policy, helping employers understand which adult-industry job posts are prohibited or allowed to keep job seekers safe. It clarifies that jobs involving sexual services, explicit interaction, or suggestive content are not permitted, while low-risk support or technical roles in adult-related businesses may be allowed, and provides steps to update or appeal removed posts.

  • Mar 23, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

Sexual-Content

Title

Adult Industry Jobs

Details

Overview

This policy applies to all employers posting jobs on Indeed. Our goal is to keep job seekers safe. Some adult industry jobs are not allowed because they may put people at risk. We may remove or hide job posts that go against this policy.

Policy requirements

Jobs are not allowed on Indeed if they:

  • Ask workers to interact sexually with customers
  • Are linked to sex work, human trafficking, or abuse
  • Use sexual or suggestive language
  • Include content that is offensive, harassing, or sexually targeted

Even if legal in the markets where the jobs are located, these high-risk jobs are not allowed on Indeed.

Examples of high-risk roles
  • Stripper
  • Dancer
  • Adult entertainer
  • Adult actor
  • Sex worker
  • Jobs using unclear titles for sexual services, like “massage” roles

Allowed adult-industry jobs

Employers may post low-risk jobs in the adult industry, such as:

  • Technical roles (for example, web engineer or video editor)
  • Retail roles (for example, cashier at an adult shop)
  • Service roles (for example, cook or janitor at an adult entertainment club)

Next steps

If we removed or reduced visibility of your job post, we advise that you:

  • Review your post considering the guidelines above.
  • Update your job to meet these guidelines.
  • Make sure the title and description are clear.

Contact Indeed Support if you believe we removed your post by mistake.

Benefits

  • The All Hiring Events tab is a central location to help you more easily prepare for your in-person, virtual, and phone interviews
  • Instead of searching for emails, you can go to the All Hiring Events tab to find details all in one place
  • Easily manage interviewers and quickly find key interview information
  • Access the check-in tool for an in-person event, or join a virtual interview

How to access the All Hiring Events tab

Important: The visibility of the Hiring Events navigation and actions depends on your permissions. If you don’t see the All hiring events tab, your account may not have the required access. Find out more in our article Adding Users and Setting Access Levels.

Via your Indeed account
  1. Sign in to your Indeed account
  2. In the left sidebar, click on Interviews
  3. Near the top of your screen, click on All Hiring Events
Via a direct link

Once logged in to your Indeed account, open the link to the Hiring Events dashboard.

Tip: Bookmark this link for faster access. You may need to log in to your Indeed account first.

Navigating your All Hiring Events tab

On the left side

On the left side, you will find your upcoming events in chronological order:

  • Event dates
  • Total estimated attendees & RSVPs
  • A preview of the job titles
  • Status of the event
  • Type of event (via icon)
On the right side

When you select an event, you’ll find more details and features, such as:

  • Join the virtual lobby (virtual events), which opens the interview lobby.
  • Manage candidates (in-person events), which opens the check-in tool.
  • Full list of job titles that open up the job posting on Indeed.
  • Interview team, as determined by the user who created the event.
  • Interview schedule, which breaks down estimated attendees or RSVPs by day and by time slot.

Assigning an Indeed Smart Sourcing subscription

Only a single user can be assigned a Smart Sourcing subscription at a time; they cannot be shared. As an account administrator, you can reassign Standard subscriptions once every 30 days and Professional subscriptions up to four times every 30 days to other linked users on the employer account. To reassign a subscription, navigate to the Subscriptions menu and click “Assign subscription” next to the plan you want to move.

A Smart Sourcing subscription lets you contact candidates with resumes on Indeed. You must assign each subscription to one user on the employer account. This guide explains how to assign a subscription to another user and who is allowed to do it.

ATS Identity Sync for Smart Screening

ATS Identity Sync automatically manages Smart Screening user access and job-level permissions in Indeed based on roles and job assignments defined in your applicant tracking system (ATS), refreshing approximately every 2 hours. This feature reduces manual permission setup in Indeed, supports standard Recruiter (read & edit) and Hiring Manager (read-only) roles, requires an active Smart Screening subscription with a supported ATS, and can be enabled or disabled with help from your Indeed representative.

  • May 12, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

ATS-Identity-Sync-for-Smart-Screening

Title

ATS Identity Sync for Smart Screening

Details

Overview

ATS Identity Sync connects your applicant tracking system (ATS) to Indeed. It helps you manage who can use Smart Screening on your team.

When ATS Identity Sync is on, Indeed uses your ATS to decide:

  • Which people can access Smart Screening
  • Which jobs they can access and edit

This means you don’t need to set up these permissions in Indeed.

Important: This feature isn’t available for all employers. Contact your Indeed representative for more information.

How ATS Identity Sync works

Managing access in more than one system can take a lot of time. ATS Identity Sync makes your ATS the single source of truth for Smart Screening permissions.

Once it is turned on, Indeed will:

  • Review the roles and job assignments in your ATS
  • Match those people to the right jobs in Smart Screening

Only the people tied to a job in your ATS will be able to find that job in Smart Screening. Admins will keep their ability to access all jobs in Smart Screening.

Key benefits
Automatic updates

User permissions are refreshed at least every two hours to keep up with changes in your ATS.

Simplified visibility

Recruiters and hiring managers can only access the jobs assigned to them.

Less manual work

You no longer need to invite users or assign Smart Screening permissions inside Indeed.

Eligibility

To join the ATS Identity Sync pilot, you must have:

  • An active Smart Screening subscription with at least one job enrolled
  • A supported ATS that already sends jobs to Indeed
  • Clear roles set in your ATS, for each job, such as Recruiter or Hiring Manager

If you aren’t sure whether you qualify, contact your Indeed representative.

How permissions work

ATS Identity Sync only controls access in the Smart Screening Hub.

It doesn’t change:

  • Your general Indeed account access
  • Permissions for other Indeed products

Permissions come from the roles set in your ATS:

Recruiter

  • Indeed permission: Read & Edit
  • What they can do: Access and change Smart Screening settings for assigned jobs

Hiring Manager

  • Indeed permission: Read Only
  • What they can do: Access Smart Screening settings for assigned jobs, but not change them

What to expect when ATS Identity Sync is enabled

When you turn on ATS Identity Sync, your ATS becomes the only way to manage Smart Screening access.

Locked permissions

You won’t be able to change Smart Screening permissions in Indeed.

New users

If someone is in your ATS but doesn’t have an Indeed account, Indeed creates a dormant account for them. They get full access after they sign in to Indeed with the same email address used in your ATS.

Sync timing

Changes you make in your ATS, such as adding or removing a recruiter from a job, will show in Indeed at least every two hours or sooner.

Setting up ATS Identity Sync

The steps to set up ATS Identity Sync depend on your ATS.

For most ATS platforms (non-Workday)
  1. Open your ATS settings.
  2. Choose your ATS and follow the prompts to enter API credentials.
  3. The connection is created almost right away.
For Workday

Setup uses a direct integration:

  • If you already use ATS Sync for Workday, your Indeed representative can help turn on ATS Identity Sync.
  • Your Workday administrator must review and approve the needed API permissions.

Supported ATS platforms

  • ADP Workforce Now (Requires paid API access)
  • Ashby
  • BambooHR
  • BreezyHR (Requires paid API access)
  • Bullhorn
  • Career Plug
  • Cornerstone On Demand
  • Crelate
  • Fountain
  • Greenhouse
  • iCIMS (Requires paid API access)
  • JazzHR
  • JobDiva
  • Jobvite ATS
  • Lever
  • Oracle Cloud HCM
  • Paycor
  • Phenom Hire
  • Pinpoint
  • Recruiterflow
  • SAP SuccessFactors
  • SmartRecruiters
  • Taleo Enterprise
  • Teamtailor
  • UKG Pro
  • UKG Ready
  • Workable
  • Workday
  • Zoho Recruit

If your ATS isn’t listed, contact your Indeed representative to learn about future support.

Important: Some platforms may need paid API access. In this case, also contact your ATS representative, as the payment will be handled within the ATS.

FAQs

Question: I removed someone from my ATS. Will ATS Identity Sync remove Smart Screening access?

Answer: Yes. If you remove someone’s access to a job in your ATS, the sync will remove their Smart Screening permissions for that job.

However, it doesn’t remove their Indeed employer account. Remove the user from your employer account to stop their access to other Indeed features.

Question: Can I use custom role titles with ATS Identity Sync?

Answer: Maybe. The sync is designed for the standard Recruiter and Hiring Manager roles. During setup, your Indeed team can check if your custom titles can be mapped to either of these roles. Some custom titles may not be supported.

Tip: Check your ATS settings to find out if you can use custom titles.

Question: Can I stop ATS Identity Sync?

Answer: Yes. If you want to disable the sync, contact your Indeed representative.

When the sync is turned off:

  • The permissions from the last sync will stay in place
  • They will not update automatically anymore
  • An administrator must change user permissions on the Users page in your Indeed employer account
Question: Do users keep their Smart Screening permissions on Indeed if they are not in the ATS?

Answer: No. If a user does not have a matching role in the ATS, their Smart Screening permissions on Indeed will be removed. If they do have a matching role in the ATS, their Smart Screening permissions on Indeed will be updated to match that role.

Question: If domain enforcement and ATS Identity Sync are both turned on, will only ATS users with allowed email domains be synced to indeed.com?

Answer: Yes. Only users from your ATS whose email addresses use a domain that is allowed under your domain enforcement policy will be synced to indeed.com. All other users will not be synced.

Bulk Uploader: Creating and Editing Campaigns

Learn how to use Indeed’s Bulk Uploader tool to efficiently create or edit multiple Sponsored Job campaigns by downloading a template, entering or updating campaign details in a spreadsheet, and uploading the file—either to build new campaigns or modify existing ones while following required formatting and field guidelines.

  • May 12, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

How-to-use-the-Bulk-Uploader-tool

Title

Bulk Uploader: Creating and Editing Campaigns

Details

Overview

If you have several Sponsored Jobs, you can set up a campaign to manage them. Use the Bulk Uploader tool to create or edit more than one campaign at a time. In this article, we’ll explain how to create or edit your campaigns. Check our guide on creating your spreadsheet for step-by-step instructions. The guide also lists the fields and values you can use.

Important: Your experience may vary depending on the Bulk Uploader and spreadsheet editor version you’re using.

Getting started

  1. Sign in to your Indeed employer account.
  2. From your Dashboard, open the Campaigns page.
  3. Go to the Campaigns tab.

Tip: Learn more about setting up a Sponsored Job campaign.

Creating a bulk campaign

Download the template
  1. Select More tools, then Bulk upload campaigns.
  2. Open the Bulk Campaign creation tab.
  3. Select Download template.
  4. Open the template in a spreadsheet editor, such as Excel or Google Sheets.
Enter and upload your data
  1. In the Bulk Uploader tool, select Input campaign details into template.
  2. Enter your campaign details in the spreadsheet. Use our spreadsheet guide for:
    1. Step-by-step instructions for Excel and Google
    2. The fields and values you can use
  3. Return to the Bulk Campaign creation tab and upload your completed template.
  4. Click or tap Continue to create your campaign.

Editing a bulk campaign

Download your campaigns
  1. Select More tools, then Bulk upload campaigns.
  2. Open the Bulk Campaign editing tab.
  3. Export your campaigns (Active, Paused, or both).
Update and upload your file
  1. Open the file in Excel or Google Sheets.
    1. Open your spreadsheet editor first, then import the CSV to prevent formatting issues.
    2. Use our spreadsheet guide for step-by-step instructions for Excel and Google Sheets.
  2. Make your updates.
    1. Use only the fields and values listed in our spreadsheet guide.
  3. Upload the file back to the Bulk Uploader.
Editing guidelines
  • Don’t edit or remove the [Campaign Key] field.
  • Only include fields you want to update. Remove unused columns to avoid accidental changes.
  • Empty cells result in no changes to your campaign.

Bulk Uploader: Creating Your Spreadsheet

Learn how to correctly create and format a Bulk Uploader spreadsheet in Excel or Google Sheets by importing the CSV template, preserving data formats, and saving it for upload, while understanding all required and optional fields, accepted values, and validation rules—covering both campaign creation and editing scenarios to avoid errors and ensure successful uploads.

  • Apr 28, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

Bulk-Uploader-Creating-Your-Spreadsheet

Title

Bulk Uploader: Creating Your Spreadsheet

Details

Overview

Use this guide when creating or editing campaigns with the Bulk Uploader tool. You’ll find the steps for creating the spreadsheet in Excel or Google Sheets. We’ll also explain each field in the template, including when to use it and which values are allowed. Some fields are only for creating campaigns, others for editing, and others or both.

Important: Only use the fields and values listed below in English. Translating them, or using unsupported values and formats, may result in errors.

Creating your spreadsheet

Excel
  1. Open a new Excel workbook.
  2. Go to the Data tab.
  3. In the Get External Data group, select From Text.
  4. Locate the CSV file you downloaded and select Get Data.
  5. In the Text Import Wizard:
    1. Choose Delimited as the original data type, then select Next.
    2. Select Comma as the delimiter and deselect Tab, then select Next.
    3. Prevent Excel from converting your formats:
      1. In Data preview, select the first column.
      2. Scroll to the last column, then select it while holding Shift to highlight all columns.
      3. Under Column data format, choose Text, then select Finish.
  6. Select OK to insert the data into cell A1.
  7. Save the Excel file as a CSV before uploading it to the Bulk Uploader tool.
Google Sheets
  1. Open Google Sheets.
  2. Select File > Import, then go to the Upload tab.
  3. Upload the CSV file you downloaded.
  4. In the import settings:
    1. Choose Import location (for example, Insert new sheet).
    2. Select Separator type.
      1. If Detect automatically doesn’t work, open the CSV in a text editor and confirm the separator. Then select that option.
    3. Set Convert text to numbers, dates and formulas to No.
  5. Select Import Data.
  6. Make your edits, then download the file again as a CSV before uploading it to the Bulk Uploader tool.

Spreadsheet fields and values

Campaign Key (editing only, required)

This is the encrypted [advertisementId] of the campaign.

  • Do not edit or remove this field when updating campaigns.
Potential errors
  • Invalid [Campaign Key]
  • This campaign cannot be edited
Campaign Name (required for creating)

The name of your campaign must be unique.

Potential errors
  • [Campaign Name] cannot be longer than 250 characters
  • [Campaign Name] is required
  • This [Campaign Name] already exists
Budget Type (creating only)

Controls whether your budget is one-time or monthly.

  • Default value: [Lifetime]
  • Accepted values:
    • [Lifetime] to set a one-time budget for the entire campaign
    • [Monthly] to set a recurring budget that renews every month
Potential errors
  • Invalid [Budget Type], please input one of [Lifetime] or [Monthly]
  • The [Budget Type] for [Scheduled Interviews] campaigns must be [Lifetime]
Budget Amount (required for creating)

The budget amount must be a whole number. It will set either the one-time budget or the monthly budget, depending on the budget type.

Potential errors
  • Invalid [Budget Amount], please input a positive number
  • [Budget Amount] is required when creating a campaign
  • Enter a valid [Budget Amount]
  • The [Budget Amount] must be greater than {0} (currency-dependent limit)
Jobs Query

Targets specific jobs within a campaign.

  • Leave empty to sponsor all jobs for the selected source
  • The % character is not supported
Potential errors
  • Jobs Query exceeded maximum query length 65535 characters
  • Invalid Jobs Query (ensure no invalid characters are used)
  • The supplied Jobs Query surpasses the 20 jobs limit for [Scheduled Interviews] campaigns
Prorate First Month (creating only, partially required)

Determines whether to prorate the first month’s budget.

  • Required if [Budget Type] is [Monthly]
  • Accepted values: [Yes], [No]
Potential errors
  • Invalid [Prorate First Month], please input one of [Yes] or [No]
  • [Prorate First Month] is required when [Budget Type] is [Monthly]
  • [Prorate First Month] is only valid when [Budget Type] is [Monthly]
Start Date and Time

Controls when the campaign begins.

  • Leave empty or use [NOW] to start immediately
  • Time is based on Central Standard Time (CST), regardless of your local time zone
Accepted formats
  • yyyy-MM-dd
  • MM/dd/yyyy
  • MM-dd-yyyy
  • yyyy-MM-dd hh:mm (to include a start time)
Potential errors
  • Invalid [Start Date], please input a valid date or NOW
  • [Start Date] cannot be earlier than now
  • [Start Date] cannot be later than [End Date]
  • [Start Date] cannot be edited after the campaign starts spending
End Date and Time (partially required)

Controls when the campaign stops spending.

  • Required if [Budget Type] is [Lifetime]
  • Must be later than the start date
  • Time is based on Central Standard Time (CST), regardless of your local time zone
Accepted formats
  • yyyy-MM-dd
  • MM/dd/yyyy
  • MM-dd-yyyy
  • yyyy-MM-dd hh:mm (to include an end time)
Special value (editing only)
  • Use [REMOVE] to clear the end date (if applicable)
Potential errors
  • Invalid [End Date], please input a valid date or REMOVE
  • [End Date] cannot be earlier than now
  • [End Date] is required when [Budget Type] is [Lifetime]
  • Cannot set [End Date] to [REMOVE] if no end date exists
Paused

Controls whether the campaign starts active or paused.

  • Default value:
    • [Yes] for Scheduled Interview campaigns
    • [No] for all others
  • Accepted values: [Yes], [No]
Potential errors
  • Invalid Paused, please input one of [Yes] or [No]
Additional Ads Reach (creating only)

Use Additional Ads Reach to turn on the Trusted Media Network (TMN). Indeed and Glassdoor are enabled by default; TMN adds more sites to your reach.

  • Default value: [No]
  • Accepted values: [Yes], [No]
Potential errors
  • Invalid [Additional Ads Reach], please input one of [Yes] or [No]
Tracking Token

A custom tracking string for your campaign.

Special value (editing only)
  • Use [REMOVE] to clear the value
Potential errors
  • Tracking Token exceeded maximum character length 255

Claiming and Using Sponsored Job Credits

This article explains how to claim, apply, and manage Indeed Sponsored Job promotional credits—whether received via email, ads, or other media—and outlines steps for new and existing accounts, including entering promo codes, auto-applying credits to Sponsored Job invoices, checking balances and expiration dates, and resolving common issues such as eligibility limits, expired offers, and account mismatches. It helps users understand all available ways to redeem credits, how credits are used on postings, and how to troubleshoot when a code cannot be claimed.

  • Jan 8, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

What-is-a-Sponsored-Job-credit

Title

Claiming and Using Sponsored Job Credits

Details

Overview

Sponsored Job credits help you save on job posts. They work like coupons, providing a discount on Sponsored Jobs. You might receive credit through a marketing email or ad. This article explains how to claim, use, and manage your credits. It also covers common issues when claiming credits.

You can also view our article about Indeed Annual Deal Sponsored Job Credits.

Claiming a credit

New accounts
  1. You may learn about promotional codes for credits via mail, on TV, radio, podcasts, social media, or other ads.
  2. Go to the landing page provided in the ad.
  3. Enter the promotional code to claim the credit and add it to your account.
Existing accounts
Marketing email
  1. You may receive promotional credits by email.
  2. Click or tap the button in the email to open the claim page. If you’re not signed into your account, you’ll be prompted to do so.
  3. Follow the steps to claim the credit and add it to your account.

Important: These claim codes are unique to you. They’ll automatically fill in on the claim page.

Other media
  1. You may receive credit through mail, TV or radio ads, podcasts, social media, or other ads.
  2. Use the URL provided in the ad.
  3. Enter the code provided to claim the credit and add it to your account.

Using credits

  1. Claim a credit to apply it to your account automatically.
  2. Post a sponsored job.
  3. We’ll discount the credit amount from the total of your next billing invoice automatically. There’s no additional action or step needed to use Sponsored Job Credits once they have been claimed on your account.

Did you know? If your job post costs less than the credits in your account, you can use any remaining credits for future job posts before they expire.

Check your available credits

To see if you have credits available to use:

  1. Sign in to your employer account on Indeed
  2. Go to your Billing page
  3. Look at the bottom right of the page for the Promotions section

If you have available credits, this section will show:

  • The credit amount available to use
  • The expiration date of your credits

If you don’t see a Promotions section, you don’t have any active credits on your account.

Confirm if credits were already used

  1. Sign in to your employer account on Indeed.
  2. Go to your Billing page.
  3. On this page, you’ll find your Transaction history.
  4. Find your transaction history on this page
  5. Select the invoice for the job post you want to check
  6. Look for a line showing promotional items on the invoice

Credit expiration dates

Important: Offer terms may vary, so always check the terms and conditions specified on your promotional offer.

New accounts
  • Your promotional credit activates when you post your first job.
  • The expiration period specified in the promotional offer’s terms and conditions starts then.
Existing accounts
  • If you’ve posted a job before, you must claim your promotional code by a specific date.
  • You can typically claim your credit up to 30 days after receiving the offer.
  • Once claimed, you generally have one year to use the credits.

Common issues when claiming credits

Eligibility requirements
  • Check the promotion’s terms to ensure you qualify.
  • Some credits are only for first-time users. You may not be eligible if you’ve used Indeed before, even if it was a long time ago.
  • You may not qualify for another credit if you already have an unused credit on your account. Check your credit balance on your Billing page.
Expired promotions

Ensure you claim the credit before the expiration date. You can’t redeem expired offers.

Promo code sharing
  • Promotional codes are unique to each user and cannot be shared or transferred.
  • If a friend wants to use a similar promotion, they should contact Indeed customer support for available options.
Company or account mismatch
  • Credits may be assigned to a shared company account or a specific employee’s Indeed account.
  • Contact Indeed support or your Client Success representative to explore transfer options.

FAQs

Question: How do I check if I have credits available?

Answer: Go to your Billing page and look for the Promotions section at the bottom right. This section shows your available credit amount and expiration date. If you don’t see this section, you don’t have active credits.

Question: How do I know if credits were used on a specific job post?

Answer: Go to your Billing page, find the invoice for that job post in your transaction history, and look for a line showing promotional items. If this line appears, it shows how much credit was used for that job post.

Question: How do I apply credits to my Sponsored Jobs?

Answer: Credits are automatically applied once you’ve claimed them. If you have credits and post a Sponsored Job, the full applicable amount of your credits will be used to pay for sponsorship. If you have credits left over, those will be applied to your next Sponsored Job.

Conviction History Disclosure Policy

This article explains Indeed’s Conviction History Disclosure Policy for New York and New Jersey job postings, helping employers understand that job posts in these states cannot include language about criminal history or background checks (with exceptions for healthcare, education, law enforcement, security, and transportation roles). If a job post is removed or hidden, employers can review and remove prohibited language to restore visibility or contact Indeed Support if they believe the removal was a mistake.

  • May 7, 2026
  • Knowledge

URL Name

Policy-Conviction-History-Disclosure

Title

Conviction History Disclosure Policy

Details

Overview

This policy addresses criminal history and background check language. It applies to job postings in New York and New Jersey.

Policy

Job posts in New York and New Jersey may not include language about criminal history or background checks.

If you post a job in New York or New Jersey, your job post should not:

  • Include statements about a background check
  • Exclude people based on criminal history
  • Say that people with convictions can’t apply

Your job will not be eligible for visibility on Indeed if it includes this type of language.

Exceptions

Some exceptions may apply. These may include jobs in:

  • Healthcare
  • Education
  • Law enforcement
  • Security
  • Transportation

Important: Employers are responsible for complying with all applicable laws on criminal history and background checks.

Next steps

If we removed or hid your job post:

  • Review your post using the guidelines above.
  • Remove any language about criminal history or background checks if the job is in New York or New Jersey.

Contact Indeed Support if you believe we removed your job by mistake.

Creating an Employer Account on Indeed

It also covers eligibility requirements, troubleshooting, security practices, and what to do if an existing company account or multiple accounts already exist.

  • Jun 16, 2026
